### [35] [URNet: A Unified Reparameterized Network for Efficient RGB-D Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05671)
*Guoan Xu,Zhengxue Wang,Yang Xiao,Ligeng Chen,Guangwei Gao,Dongchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出URNet（统一重参数化RGB-D分割网络），在单一编码器内同时完成多模态特征提取与跨模态融合，配以线性门控注意力（LGA）和金字塔合并解码器（PMD），在多个RGB-D分割基准上实现SOTA性能与高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-D语义分割方法通常采用双编码器分别处理RGB和深度输入，导致深度表征捕获不充分、跨模态交互受限，且额外编码器分支引入冗余计算，阻碍轻量化部署。

Method: 1) 采用重参数化策略压缩网络结构，加速推理；2) 在每个Reparameterized Block（RepBlock）中引入线性门控注意力（LGA）模块，在不同特征尺度上充分利用RGB和深度互补线索；3) 设计精简高效的金字塔合并解码器（PMD）以弥补现有研究中解码器设计的不足。

Result: 在多个RGB-D语义分割基准上的大量实验表明，URNet在保持高效率的同时取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: URNet通过统一编码器设计有效地消除了双编码器的冗余计算，同时通过重参数化、LGA和PMD的有效结合，在分割精度与推理效率之间取得了良好平衡，验证了单编码器RGB-D分割范式的可行性。

Abstract: Previous RGB-D semantic segmentation methods commonly employ dual encoders to separately process RGB and depth inputs, followed by dedicated modules for cross-modal feature fusion. However, such designs often inadequately capture depth representations and consequently limit effective cross-modal interaction, while the additional encoder branch introduces redundant computation that hinders lightweight execution. To tackle these challenges, we propose URNet, a Unified Reparameterized RGB-D Network that performs simultaneous multi-modal feature extraction and cross-modal fusion within a single encoder. Specifically, we adopt a reparameterization strategy to compact the network architecture and facilitate fast inference. Within each Reparameterized Block (RepBlock), a Linear Gated Attention (LGA) module is introduced to fully exploit complementary RGB and depth cues across different feature scales. Furthermore, considering that decoder design has been relatively underexplored in existing RGB-D segmentation models, we develop a concise yet effective universal decoder, termed the Pyramid Merging Decoder (PMD). Extensive experiments on multiple RGB-D segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that URNet achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining high efficiency. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wild-Stephen/URNet.

</details>


### [36] [DistMedVL: Distributional Vision-Language Alignment for Uncertainty-Aware Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05683)
*Jiaxuan Li,Qing Xu,Xiangjian He,Yue Li,Daokun Zhang,Fiseha B. Tesema,Rong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DistMedVL是一个概率视觉-语言分割框架，通过轻量级PCM-Adapter在冻结编码器基础上显式建模表征不确定性，在八个医学分割基准上以仅6.3M可训练参数超越最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言分割方法依赖确定性跨模态匹配，忽略了医学图像中模糊边界带来的偶然不确定性和有限训练数据带来的认知不确定性，导致在域偏移下性能脆弱。

Method: 提出DistMedVL框架，包含两个顺序模块：(1) Mahalanobis对齐模块(MAM)，将文本标记建模为高斯分布，通过马氏距离计算patch-文本兼容性，实现方差条件化的匹配以降低不可靠特征维度的权重；(2) 分布流模块(DFM)，估计模态置信度参数并执行视觉引导的文本分布精炼，以适应跨成像模态的分布变化。整体仅引入6.3M可训练参数，在冻结编码器之上运行。

Result: 在八个医学分割基准上，DistMedVL超越了当前最先进方法，表现出优越的数据效率、扰动鲁棒性和跨数据集泛化能力，且参数量极少。

Conclusion: 概率建模跨模态表征不确定性是提升医学图像视觉-语言分割鲁棒性和泛化能力的有效途径，DistMedVL展示了在轻量化条件下同时处理偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment of visual and textual representations is fundamental to multimodal medical image understanding, yet remains hindered by uncertainty in both modalities under real-world clinical conditions. Existing vision-language segmentation methods rely on deterministic cross-modal matching, which overlooks aleatoric uncertainty from ambiguous boundaries and epistemic uncertainty from limited training data, leading to fragile performance under domain shift. To address this issue, we propose DistMedVL, a probabilistic vision-language framework that introduces a lightweight Probabilistic Cross-Modal Adapter (PCM-Adapter) upon frozen encoders to explicitly model representational uncertainty. Specifically, the PCM-Adapter comprises two sequential modules for progressive probabilistic alignment. We first devise a Mahalanobis Alignment Module (MAM) that models textual tokens as Gaussian distributions and computes patch-text compatibility via Mahalanobis distance, yielding variance-conditioned matching that downweights unreliable feature dimensions. Moreover, we devise a Distribution Flow Module (DFM) that estimates modality-wise confidence parameters and performs vision-guided refinement of textual distributions, accommodating distributional variation across imaging modalities. Extensive experiments across eight medical segmentation benchmarks demonstrate that DistMedVL outperforms state-of-the-art methods with only 6.3M trainable parameters, exhibiting superior data efficiency, perturbation robustness and cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [37] [SciQNet: Two-Stage Multimodal Adaptation for Scientific Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05691)
*Yin-Loon Khor,Yi-Jie Wong,Jing Jie Tan,Ming Jie Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciQNet，一个两阶段多模态自适应框架用于科学图像质量评估（SIQA）。第一阶段在科学文档图像上进行领域自适应预训练，第二阶段通过联合评分与理解监督进行任务微调。实验表明预训练数据的相关性比规模更重要，最终模型在ICME 2026 SIQA挑战赛评分赛道排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图像与自然图像不同，其质量同时取决于视觉清晰度和科学信息性，这使得评估更具挑战性。现有方法难以同时兼顾这两个维度，需要一个专门针对科学图像特点设计的质量评估框架。

Method: SciQNet采用两阶段多模态适配框架：第一阶段在科学文档图像上进行领域自适应预训练；第二阶段结合评分导向和理解导向的监督进行任务微调。评分监督采用指令微调与基于评分词logits的Huber损失相结合；理解监督则构建为多选视觉问答任务。

Result: 使用40%分层子集的领域自适应数据达到最佳性能，表明预训练数据相关性比规模更重要。最终模型SIQA-S得分为92.21，SIQA-U得分为47.38，综合得分69.80。在ICME 2026科学图像质量评估挑战赛评分赛道排名第二。

Conclusion: SciQNet通过两阶段多模态适配有效解决了科学图像质量评估中的视觉清晰度与科学信息性联合建模问题，同时揭示了预训练数据相关性可能比规模更重要的发现，为科学图像质量评估提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific images are essential for communicating experimental observations, quantitative evidence and conceptual knowledge. Unlike natural images, their quality depends on both visual clarity and scientific informativeness, making assessment challenging. In this work, we present SciQNet, a two-stage multimodal adaptation framework for scientific image quality assessment. The first stage performs domain-adaptive pretraining on scientific document images and the second stage conducts task-specific fine-tuning with joint scoring and understanding supervision. For scoring-oriented supervision, we combine instruction tuning with a Huber loss derived from rating-word logits, while understanding-oriented supervision is formulated as multiple-choice visual question answering. Experiments show that using a 40% stratified subset of the domain-adaptive data gives the best performance among the evaluated pretraining fractions, suggesting that pretraining-data relevance may be as important as pretraining-data scale. The final model achieves an SIQA-S score of 92.21, an SIQA-U score of 47.38 and a combined score of 69.80. This work presents our solution to the ICME 2026 Scientific Image Quality Assessment Challenge, which ranked 2nd in the scoring track.

</details>


### [38] [TAU-Bench: From Anomaly Instance Tracking to Fine-Grained Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05699)
*Kepeng Yang,Dongxuan Liu,Rongxin Gao,Zixin Su,Rui Wu,Shuzhao Xie,Chenxin Li,Panwang Pan,Yuzhi Huang,Yue Huang,Jingyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAU-Bench，一个以轨迹为中心的基准，用于联合评估视频异常理解中的异常实例跟踪与细粒度语义理解。基准包含1,118个视频、1,454条轨迹和202,438个像素级掩码，覆盖49种事件和45种场景类别。评估发现，现有视觉语言模型虽能生成合理的异常解释，但常无法可靠地定位和跟踪正确的异常实例，揭示了语义推理与视觉锚定之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类理解异常事件是一个连贯的感知过程，需要识别焦点实例、跟踪其行为并解释其为何违反场景预期。虽然现有视觉语言模型能生成语义流畅的异常描述，但其流畅性不能保证解释在时间上始终锚定于正确的异常实例。现有基准将跟踪和语义理解分开评估，导致实例-语义不一致问题未被测量。

Method: 构建TAU-Bench基准，包含1,118个视频、1,454条轨迹和202,438个像素级掩码，覆盖49种事件和45种场景类别。配备以轨迹为中心的标注，将实例级识别、事件级理解和场景级推理连接起来。为规模化构建，设计了自动化数据引擎，包括异常适宜性过滤、异常实例轨迹构建、分层字幕标注和人工质量控制。

Result: 对代表性视觉语言模型家族的评估表明，产生合理异常解释的模型仍可能无法可靠地定位和跟踪正确实例，揭示了语义推理与视觉锚定之间的持续差距。

Conclusion: 实例锚定的评估是迈向更真实、更可靠视频异常理解系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Humans understand anomalous events through a coherent perceptual process in which they identify the focal instance, follow its behavior as the event unfolds, and interpret why it violates the expectations of the surrounding scene. Video anomaly understanding (VAU) seeks to endow models with a similar capability, moving beyond deciding whether a video is anomalous toward explaining how the event develops and why it matters. Although recent vision--language models (VLMs) can generate detailed and plausible anomaly descriptions, their semantic fluency does not ensure that these interpretations remain grounded in the correct anomaly instance over time. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate tracking and semantic understanding through separate protocols, leaving such instance--semantic inconsistency largely unmeasured. We therefore introduce TAU-Bench, a track-centric benchmark for jointly evaluating anomaly instance tracking and fine-grained anomaly understanding. TAU-Bench contains 1,118 videos, 1,454 tracks, and 202,438 pixel-level masks spanning 49 event and 45 scene categories, together with track-centric annotations that connect instance-level identification, event-level understanding, and scene-level reasoning. To build TAU-Bench at scale, we developed an automated data engine integrating anomaly suitability filtering, anomaly instance track construction, hierarchical caption annotation, and human quality control. Evaluations across representative VLM families show that models producing plausible anomaly interpretations may still fail to localize and track the correct instance reliably, revealing a persistent gap between semantic reasoning and visual grounding. These findings therefore highlight instance-grounded evaluation as an important step toward more faithful and reliable VAU systems.

</details>


### [39] [StreamArena: Toward Continuous, Interactive, and Long-Horizon Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05703)
*Xichen Zhang,Guankai Li,Yinghao Zhu,Shijian Wang,Sitong Wu,Shaozuo Yu,Meng Chu,Yuan Lu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamArena基准和StreamMind架构，用于小时级交互式流媒体视频理解。StreamArena包含243个全长视频和3,646个开放式问答对，评估实时感知、历史回溯、主动交互和多模态工具利用四种能力。实验揭示连续交互与长时程多模态理解之间存在张力，StreamMind通过双层架构（前端工人负责低延迟交互，后端工人异步构建持久多模态记忆）解决此问题，在全部四项能力上超越现有流式基线并降低查询到答案的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主多模态智能体评估主要依赖短视频片段和多项选择题形式，存在两个严重缺陷：一是仅处理最后四帧的简单基线即可媲美复杂流式模型，二是选项暴露语言捷径。这无法准确评估智能体在真实连续环境中的小时级流媒体理解和交互能力。

Method: 首先构建StreamArena基准，包含243个平均时长88.8分钟的全长视频和3,646个严格标注的开放式问答对，覆盖实时感知、历史回溯、主动交互和多模态工具利用四个维度。其次提出StreamMind双层架构：前端工人独立调度处理延迟关键型交互和主动监控任务；后端工人异步构建持久多模态记忆，并执行历史回溯和外部搜索。

Result: 评估发现：仅保留最近帧的方法无法恢复遥远事件，将历史观察转为文本的方法丢失视觉证据，反复压缩视觉记忆的方法难以保留细粒度细节。StreamMind在所有四项能力上均超越现有流式基线，并通过重用持久状态降低了查询到答案的延迟。

Conclusion: 连续交互与长时程多模态理解之间存在根本性张力，StreamArena为暴露这一问题提供了有效评估基准，StreamMind通过将延迟关键任务与持久记忆构建解耦的两层架构，为该问题提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous multimodal agents in continuous, real-world environments requires them to ingest unbounded audio-visual streams and maintain hour-scale memory. However, current evaluations predominantly rely on brief clips and multiple-choice formats. This design allows minimal baselines that process only the last four frames to match or surpass complex streaming models, while answer options also expose language shortcuts. We introduce StreamArena, a benchmark for hour-scale, interactive streaming video understanding. StreamArena contains 243 full-length videos averaging 88.8 minutes and 3,646 rigorously annotated, open-ended question-answer pairs that evaluate real-time perception, historical retrospection, proactive interaction, and multimodal tool utilization. Evaluation across diverse systems exposes a tension between continuous interaction and long-horizon multimodal comprehension. Methods that retain only recent frames cannot recover distant events, methods that convert past observations into text lose visual evidence, and methods that repeatedly compress visual memory struggle to preserve fine-grained details over time. We address this tension with StreamMind, a two-tier architecture that assigns latency-critical interaction and proactive monitoring to independently scheduled frontend workers, while backend workers asynchronously construct persistent multimodal memory and perform historical recall and external search. StreamMind outperforms existing streaming baselines across all four capabilities and reduces query-to-answer latency by reusing persistent state.

</details>


### [40] [G$^2$ARD-GS: Geometry-Guided Anchor-Regularized Gaussian Splatting Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05704)
*Puyuan Zhang,Jianming Huang,Wenkai Ye,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G²ARD-GS是一种几何引导的蒸馏方法，将稠密的彩色LiDAR高斯先验（训练无关的点云提升或已训练的GS模型）压缩为紧凑可复用的3DGS表示，在MatrixCity上实现最佳压缩性能，并在冻结几何复用场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密彩色LiDAR地图虽能提供精确的城市级几何，但直接提升为3DGS会保留数百万个图元，导致存储、传输、渲染和适配成本高昂。激进的图元削减虽能减轻负担，但会破坏新视角合成和下游几何应用所需的局部表面支撑，亟需一种在保持几何质量的同时实现高压缩的方法。

Method: G²ARD-GS采用多阶段策略：(1) 将稠密高斯先验（训练无关点云提升或已训练的GS模型）逐步整合为表面感知的紧凑代表；(2) 在构建期锚定约束下，于固定拓扑上恢复外观，恢复过程中不增删任何图元；(3) 在有限监督下，通过几何感知的视角选择来分配可用的视角预算。

Result: 在MatrixCity数据集上，G²ARD-GS在5×–30×压缩预算下取得最佳PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS，相比PUP在PSNR上提升3.2–6.8 dB。作为冻结几何复用时，离轨迹外观适配比PUP 3D-GS改善3.7–4.9 dB，并在30×压缩下保持Cambridge KingsCollege的图像到模型配准精度。

Conclusion: G²ARD-GS证明了通过几何引导的蒸馏与表面感知的图元整合，可以在大幅压缩3DGS的同时保持甚至提升新视角合成质量和几何可用性，兼顾紧凑性、可复用性与压缩后的稳定性。

Abstract: Dense colored LiDAR maps provide accurate city-scale geometry, but lifting them into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) retains millions of primitives, making the resulting models costly to store, transmit, render, and adapt. Aggressive primitive reduction alleviates this burden, but can remove the local surface support needed for stable novel-view synthesis and downstream geometric use. We introduce G$^2$ARD-GS, a geometry-guided distillation method that converts a dense Gaussian prior instantiated either as a training-free point-cloud lift or a trained GS model into a compact, reusable representation. G$^2$ARD-GS progressively consolidates the prior into surface-aware representatives, then recovers appearance on the resulting fixed topology under construction-time anchor constraints, with no primitives added or removed during recovery. Under limited supervision, geometry-aware view selection allocates the available view budget. On MatrixCity, G$^2$ARD-GS achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS across matched $5\times$--$30\times$ compression budgets, outperforming PUP by $3.2$--$6.8$,dB in PSNR. When reused as frozen geometry, the compact model improves off-trajectory appearance adaptation by $3.7$--$4.9$,dB over PUP 3D-GS and preserves image-to-model registration accuracy on Cambridge KingsCollege at $30\times$ compression. Project page: https://patrick1159.github.io/gardGS-page/.

</details>


### [41] [LAWM-3D: Learning 3D-Aware Latent Actions from Human Videos for Generalizable Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05706)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiale Zhange,Jiawei Li,Hang Guo,Wen Huang,Jinpeng Wang,Peidong Liu,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LAWM-3D，通过多视角不变统一动作标记、几何对齐约束和非注入式RGB-D联合重建目标三个紧密耦合的设计，使潜在动作模型（LAM）学习到3D感知的潜在动作，从而提升世界模型在生成质量、物理一致性和泛化能力上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型（LAM）主要依赖单视角输入并在2D像素空间中操作，简单地将多视角视频引入LAM训练无法获得3D感知的潜在动作，原因是未来帧外观泄漏、相机间外观差异和视角变化等问题。这限制了LAM在开放世界具身智能中的应用。

Method: 提出LAWM-3D框架，包含三个紧密耦合的关键设计：(1)多视角不变统一动作标记化方案，用于学习3D感知的潜在动作；(2)几何对齐约束，将中间编码器特征锚定到预训练3D基础模型，显式提供跨视角几何对应关系；(3)非注入式RGB-D联合重建目标，防止从未来帧外观信息中进行捷径学习，迫使LAM将监督聚焦于具有几何意义的运动线索。该方法采用大规模人类视频预训练加机器人微调的两阶段范式。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的3D感知潜在动作显著提升了世界模型性能，在生成质量、物理一致性和泛化能力上均达到最先进（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: 通过将几何感知能力注入潜在动作学习过程，LAWM-3D有效解决了多视角LAM训练中的根本性挑战，证明了3D感知潜在动作能够显著提升世界模型在开放世界具身智能任务中的表现。

Abstract: World models enable agents to perform forward rollout and planning without real-world interaction. However, their application in open-world embodied intelligence remains limited by the high cost of action annotations and the heterogeneity of action spaces across platforms. Recently, latent action models (LAMs) have alleviated this bottleneck by learning action representations directly from unlabeled human videos in a self-supervised manner. Nevertheless, most existing LAMs rely on single-view inputs and operate primarily in 2D pixel space, raising a fundamental question: can simply incorporating multi-view videos into LAM training endow the learned latent actions with 3D-aware perception? Our study shows that the answer is negative. The primary reasons lie in future-frame appearance leakage as well as inter-camera appearance discrepancies and viewpoint variations. To address these issues, we propose LAWM-3D, which introduces three tightly coupled key designs: (1) a multi-view invariant unified action tokenization scheme for learning 3D-aware latent actions; (2) a geometric alignment constraint that anchors intermediate encoder features to a pretrained 3D foundation model, thereby explicitly providing cross-view geometric correspondences; and (3) a non-injective RGB-D joint reconstruction objective that prevents shortcut learning from future-frame appearance information, forcing the LAM to focus supervision on motion cues with geometric significance. Importantly, these components are not simply stacked but are tightly coupled through a unified motivation. Built upon a two-stage paradigm of large-scale human video pretraining followed by robot fine-tuning, extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed 3D-aware latent actions significantly improve world model performance, achieving SOTA results in generation quality, physical consistency, and generalization ability.

</details>


### [42] [One Ranking, Any Budget: Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context Frame Selection for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05707)
*Wang Chen,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Jinfa Huang,Xiawu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context (MEC)帧选择方法，将长视频帧选择建模为Matryoshka排序问题，通过构建可复用的稀疏视频索引和位置自适应排序，实现单个排序可用于任意预算的帧选择，在保持精度的同时显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频帧选择方法为每个预定义预算优化孤立的帧子集，当预算变化时需要重新运行选择器；而按固定分数排序的方法虽然支持前缀复用，但忽略了不同排序位置的不同作用。实际应用中帧预算随下游模型、推理需求和延迟约束而变化，因此需要支持多预算的实用帧选择器。

Method: MEC框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建可复用的稀疏视频索引，避免对所有帧进行密集采样；(2) 通过稀疏探测和局部缩放（sparse probing and local zooming）发现候选帧；(3) 贪心构建位置自适应排序——早期位置集中查询条件证据，后续位置逐渐偏向时间覆盖并保持视觉多样性。该单排序可直接截断到任意目标预算，无需重新运行选择器。

Result: 在四个基准和六个帧预算上，MEC相比均匀采样平均准确率提升3.77个百分点，达到与强状态最先进选择器相当的性能，并将端到端选择延迟降低47.37%-51.19%。

Conclusion: MEC通过将帧选择形式化为Matryoshka排序问题，实现了单次排序支持多预算的帧选择，既解决了固定分数排序忽略位置角色的问题，又避免了多预算重复计算的开销，在效率和效果之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Frame selection is essential for applying Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) to long videos due to severe frame redundancy and limited context windows. Since the appropriate frame budget varies with the downstream LMM, reasoning demands, and latency constraints, a practical selector should serve multiple budgets. However, existing methods typically optimize an isolated frame subset for each predefined budget: when the budget changes, previously selected evidence may be replaced rather than progressively augmented. Ranking frames by a fixed score would allow prefix reuse across budgets, but it ignores the distinct roles of different ranking positions. In this paper, we formulate long-video frame selection as a Matryoshka ranking problem: constructing a single priority sequence whose small prefixes concentrate query-conditioned evidence, while progressively larger prefixes preserve this evidence and add broader temporal context. Efficiently constructing such a ranking is itself challenging, as densely sampling long videos and evaluating frame-query relevance incurs substantial overhead. We therefore introduce Matryoshka Evidence-to-Context (MEC) Frame Selection, a training-free framework that builds a reusable sparse video index, discovers candidates through sparse probing and local zooming, and greedily constructs a position-adaptive ranking: early positions emphasize evidence; later positions progressively favor temporal coverage while preserving visual diversity. A single ranking can thus be truncated to any target budget without rerunning the selector. Across four benchmarks and six frame budgets, MEC improves average accuracy over uniform sampling by 3.77 percentage points, matches strong state-of-the-art selectors, and reduces end-to-end selection latency by 47.37-51.19%.

</details>


### [43] [Iterative Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Viewpoint Planning for UAV Photogrammetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05718)
*Alan Grech,Daniel Pisani,Andre Grima,Carl James Debono,Saviour Formosa,Dylan Seychell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于代理重建的迭代式混合离散-连续视点规划方法，用于无人机摄影测量的目标化采集。该方法通过摄影测量启发式评分、CMA-ES优化和视点冗余剔除，结合近景细节视点与全局覆盖视点，在三个合成场景上相比既有方法提升了重建精度与完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机摄影测量需要满足足够的表面覆盖、影像重叠、视差和分辨率的相机网络，但传统飞行模式往往不适应场景几何，导致局部重建误差。现有方法缺乏针对场景几何自适应的视点规划能力。

Method: 提出迭代式混合离散-连续视点规划方法：(1) 基于代理重建对采样表面点使用正对性、成像距离、视差和多视角观测数等摄影测量启发式进行评分；(2) 评估完整视点集的可见性、两两重叠和图连通性；(3) 围绕弱观测区域生成候选视点；(4) 使用聚类CMA-ES优化进行视点精细化；(5) 剔除冗余视点；(6) 将近景细节视点与全局模型覆盖视点组合为最终飞行路径。

Result: 在三个合成场景上的评估表明，与既有无人机路径规划方法相比，所提方法同时提升了重建精度和重建完整性。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合离散-连续视点规划方法能够有效平衡局部重建质量与全局影像网络鲁棒性，为目标化无人机摄影测量提供了一种优于传统方法的解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) photogrammetry requires camera networks that provide sufficient surface coverage, image overlap, parallax, and resolution, yet conventional flight patterns are often poorly adapted to scene geometry resulting in local reconstruction errors. This paper proposes an iterative hybrid discrete-continuous viewpoint planning method for targeted UAV photogrammetry from a proxy reconstruction. The method scores sampled surface points using photogrammetric heuristics based on frontality, imaging distance, parallax, and multi-view observation count, while also evaluating the full viewpoint set in terms of visibility, pairwise overlap, and graph connectivity. Candidate viewpoints are generated around weakly observed regions, refined using clustered Covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy (CMA-ES) optimisation, and removed when redundant. The final flight path combines close-range detail viewpoints with wider model-coverage viewpoints, balancing local reconstruction quality with global image-network robustness. Evaluation on three synthetic scenes shows that the proposed method improves both reconstruction accuracy and completeness compared with prior UAV path-planning methods.

</details>


### [44] [PhyLatent: Learning Dynamics-Relevant Representations for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05720)
*Xi Zeng,Haojie Ren,Ziying Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyLatent，一种面向动力学相关的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）世界模型的训练目标。针对全局潜在空间不塌缩但物理状态与动作后果无法保留的三大失效模式（物理不变性塌缩、物理可辨识性塌缩和反事实动力学塌缩），通过五个训练通路加以解决，在多个基准上显著降低了失效率并提升了模型预测控制成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到仅仅防止JEPA世界模型中的全局潜在表征塌缩（global latent collapse）并不足以保证表征能够保留物理状态和动作后果。现有方法可能产生物理状态信息的丢失、动作-状态对应关系模糊等问题，因此需要专门的动力学相关训练目标。

Method: PhyLatent通过三条训练通路解决三个失效模式：1）物理不变性（physical invariance）——通过静态视觉不变性和物理状态锚定确保物理状态的表征；2）物理可辨识性（physical identifiability）——通过未来表征对齐和物理状态锚定确保动作后果可区分；3）反事实动力学（counterfactual dynamics）——通过反事实分支分离和潜在空间去噪实现。具体实现包含物理状态锚定、未来表征对齐、静态视觉不变性、反事实分支分离和潜在去噪五个组件。

Result: 在OGBench-Cube上，三种失效率分别从15.60%、6.71%、8.41%降至7.53%、0.95%和4.62%，MPC成功率从70.0%提升至78.1%。在相同架构和规划器下，TwoRooms任务成功率从81.0%提升至98.0%，在Reacher和PushT上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 全局非塌缩（global non-collapse）本身不足以学习可靠的JEPA世界模型状态空间，专门针对动力学相关性的训练目标是必要的。

Abstract: We propose PhyLatent, a dynamics-relevant training objective for JointEmbedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models. Our key observation is that preventing global latent collapse does not ensure that a representation preserves physical states and action consequences. We identify three failure modes in JEPA world models: physical invariance collapse, physical identifiability collapse, and counterfactual dynamics collapse. PhyLatent addresses them through three training pathways: physical invariance, physical identifiability, and counterfactual dynamics, implemented with physical state grounding, future representation alignment, static visual invariance, counterfactual branch separation, and latent denoising. On OGBench-Cube, PhyLatent reduces the three failure rates from 15.60%, 6.71%, and 8.41% to 7.53%, 0.95%, and 4.62%, respectively, and improves model predictive control (MPC) success from 70.0% to 78.1%. With the same architecture and planner, it further improves success from 81.0% to 98.0% on TwoRooms and remains competitive on Reacher and PushT. These results show that global non-collapse alone is insufficient for learning a reliable JEPA worldmodel state space.

</details>


### [45] [Engram-E2VID: Reference-Based Event-to-Video Reconstruction via Generative Activation of Appearance Engrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05728)
*Feiyu Ji,Xiang Li,Hao Ma,Tianxiang Huang,Qingxin Lu,Mengqi Ji,Lei Han,Xiaokang Yang,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Engram-E2VID，一种基于结构引导的参考式事件到视频重建框架，通过将参考帧编码为外观记忆（engrams）并用事件流生成运动结构脚手架，在一阶扩散骨干中逐步激活相关外观token，实现无需像素级对应的高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机虽然提供细粒度的时序信息，但只编码稀疏异步的对数亮度变化而非绝对外观，导致从参考帧和事件流重建目标帧具有本质困难。核心挑战在于将事件衍生的目标时刻结构与参考帧的相关外观信息关联起来，尤其在复杂运动和长时序间隔场景下。

Method: 将参考帧编码为token空间的外观记忆（appearance engrams），同时将事件流与参考上下文变换为目标时刻的运动结构脚手架（motion-structure scaffold），捕获运动边界与事件诱导的结构变化。在一阶扩散骨干中，脚手架派生的结构token跨层渐进地与相关外观记忆交互和激活，实现token空间的关联，无需依赖像素级对应，扩散先验补充不确定或新暴露区域。

Result: 在三个基准上，Engram-E2VID相比最强同输入基线PSNR最高提升3.29 dB，LPIPS最多降低0.08，且随着重建间隔增大性能下降更缓慢。

Conclusion: 结构引导的token空间外观激活策略能够有效弥合事件稀疏信息与参考外观之间的鸿沟，在长间隔和复杂运动下保持更稳健的重建质量。

Abstract: Reference-based event-to-video reconstruction aims to recover target RGB frames from a reference frame and the event stream captured over the reference-to-target interval. Although events provide fine-grained temporal cues, they encode sparse and asynchronous log-intensity changes rather than absolute appearance, making faithful reconstruction intrinsically challenging. The central challenge lies in associating event-derived target-time structures with relevant appearance information from the reference frame, especially under complex motion and long temporal intervals. In this work, we propose Engram-E2VID, a structure-guided framework that reconstructs target frames through the generative activation of appearance engrams. Specifically, the reference frame is encoded into token-space appearance engrams, while the event stream and reference context are transformed into a target-time motion-structure scaffold that captures motion boundaries and event-induced structural changes. Within a one-step diffusion backbone, scaffold-derived structural tokens progressively interact with and activate relevant appearance engrams across layers. This token-space association allows target structures to access reference appearance without relying on direct pixel-wise correspondence, while the diffusion prior complements uncertain or newly revealed regions. Across three benchmarks, Engram-E2VID improves PSNR by up to 3.29 dB and reduces LPIPS by up to 0.08 over the strongest same-input baseline, while degrading more slowly as the reconstruction interval increases.

</details>


### [46] [LiteKD-Net: Lightweight Knowledge-Distilled Network for Mobile Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05739)
*Zhou Zhiyi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiteKD-Net，一种针对移动端图像去噪的轻量级知识蒸馏网络，通过物理引导的噪声模拟、轻量级Student网络和特征级知识蒸馏，在保证恢复质量的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端图像去噪需要兼顾恢复质量和低计算成本，但收集大规模LQ-GT干净配对的训练数据非常繁琐，因此需要一个既轻量又无需大量真实配对数据的去噪方案。

Method: 1) 采用物理引导的噪声模拟管线，通过添加像素串扰生成配对训练数据；2) 将Real-ESRGAN适配到同分辨率去噪任务，并使用基于深度可分离卷积的Lite-RRDB块构建轻量级Student网络；3) 在特征层面应用知识蒸馏，将Teacher的恢复能力转移到Student，且不增加额外推理成本。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，模型运行时间大幅减少，推理速度提升，同时保持良好的恢复质量；在各项指标上均优于SwinIR。

Conclusion: LiteKD-Net在恢复质量与计算效率之间取得了良好的平衡，为移动端图像去噪提供了一种有效的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile image denoising requires both good restoration quality and low computational cost. In addition, it's annoying to collect large-scale LQ-GT clean pairs. As a result, we propose LiteKD-Net, a lightweight knowledge-distilled network for mobile image denoising. First, a physics-guided noise simulation pipeline generates paired training data by adding pixel crosstalk compared with pipelines applied to cameras. Next, we adapt the Real-ESRGAN to identity-resolution denoising and construct a lightweight Student using Lite-RRDB blocks based on depthwise separable convolutions. Third, feature-level knowledge distillation is applied to transfer the Teacher's restoration capability to the Student without introducing additional inference cost. Experiments on real-world datasets show that our model reaches great reduction in runtime and increase in the inference rate with good restoration quality. Our model also reaches the best in all metrics compared with SwinIR. These results indicate that LiteKD-Net provides a great trade-off between restoration quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [47] [ConceptADapt: Concept-guided Adaptive Feature Reconstruction with Dynamic Attention for Few-Shot Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05743)
*Yufei Li,Yicheng Ruan,Long Tian,Dongsheng Wang,Liang Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConceptADapt，一种概念引导的自适应特征重建模型，用于小样本工业异常检测（FS-IAD）。通过预先学习固定正常概念并结合动态注意力机制与稀疏自编码器来缓解低数据场景下的特征捷径问题，同时利用LoRA实现轻量级快速推理适配。在MVTec-AD、VisA和MPDD三个基准上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Few-shot异常检测中，虽然基础模型的特征表示能力强大，但由于正常训练样本极度稀缺，模型泛化能力仍然脆弱。此外，低数据体制下特征捷径问题（feature shortcut problem）尤为严重，限制了模型的检测性能。

Method: 1）预学习一组固定正常概念，利用其挖掘与查询特征的关系，重新校准查询特征统计量用于异常检测；2）提出动态注意力机制与稀疏自编码器相结合，在训练中学习鲁棒的正常概念，缓解特征捷径问题；3）将LoRA集成到注意力模块中，使推理阶段仅有极少的更新参数，实现快速适配。

Result: 在MVTec-AD、VisA和MPDD三个广泛采用的FS-IAD基准上，该模型在异常检测和定位任务中均持续优于现有SOTA方法，并在多种shot设置下取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: ConceptADapt通过概念引导的特征重建与动态注意力机制，有效解决了小样本工业异常检测中数据稀缺导致的泛化脆弱问题，同时以轻量级设计保证了推理效率，为FS-IAD任务提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Few-shot industrial anomaly detection (FS-IAD) focuses on detecting and localizing visual defects in industrial inspection during the cold-start phase, where only a limited number of normal training samples are available per category. Recent advances in this field predominantly leverage visual features from foundation-model and have achieved promising performance. Despite the strong representational power of foundation-model features, the model generalization remains fragile due to the extreme scarcity of normal training data.To address this pivotal issue, we propose ConceptADapt, a concept-guided adaptive feature reconstruction model with dynamic attention. Specifically, our model pre-learns a set of fixed normal concepts from the limited support features and leverages them to mine relationships with query features, thereby recalibrating their statistics for improved anomaly detection at test time. To mitigate the prevalent feature shortcut problem, which is particularly severe under low-data regimes, we further develop a dynamic attention mechanism integrated with sparse autoencoders to learn robust normal concepts during training. Moreover, to enable fast adaptation during inference, our model remains lightweight by incorporating LoRA into the attention module, which introduces only minimal updating parameters.Extensive experiments on three widely adopted FS-IAD benchmarks, including MVTec-AD, VisA, and MPDD, demonstrate that our model consistently outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches across both detection and localization tasks, achieving significant improvements under various shot settings.

</details>


### [48] [UniVVT: A Unified End-to-End Framework for High-Fidelity Video Virtual Try-on](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05745)
*Yushe Cao,Shikun Feng,Fei Shen,Haikuo Peng,Jianqiang Xia,Yiheng Zhu,Dianxi Shi,Chun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniVVT 提出一种统一的端到端视频虚拟试穿框架，将任务重构为语义条件视频生成，消除了传统方法中的掩码、姿态和形变等显式几何先验模块，通过多模态大语言模型构建的场景-任务感知器实现隐式语义引导，并以三阶段渐进训练策略耦合各组件，在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法普遍采用掩码条件视频修复的多阶段流水线，依赖独立的人体解析、姿态估计和服装形变模块。这种设计不仅部署复杂，且显式几何先验的误差会不可逆地传播到生成视频中，影响最终质量。

Method: UniVVT 将虚拟试穿重构为语义条件视频生成任务。核心由三部分构成：(1) 基于多模态大语言模型的场景-任务感知器，联合编码源视频、目标服装和任务指令为紧凑的任务感知潜在标记，隐式捕获迁移内容及迁移方式；(2) 轻量级语义桥，将潜在标记与扩散视频生成器的条件空间对齐；(3) 三阶段渐进训练策略，依次进行语义对齐、联合任务适应和灵活分辨率精化，以鲁棒地耦合异构组件。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，UniVVT 达到了最先进的性能水平，验证了隐式语义引导是端到端虚拟试穿中脆弱几何预处理的简单有效替代方案。

Conclusion: UniVVT 证明了通过隐式语义令牌替代显式几何先验，可以构建简洁、稳健且部署友好的统一端到端虚拟试穿框架，为视频虚拟试穿提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) synthesizes a video of a person wearing a target garment while preserving identity, motion, and scene dynamics. Dominant approaches cast VVT as mask-conditioned video inpainting and rely on separate modules for human parsing, pose estimation, and garment warping. This multi-stage design complicates deployment and, more critically, allows errors in explicit geometric priors to propagate irreversibly into the generated video. We present UniVVT, a unified end-to-end framework that reframes VVT as semantically conditioned video generation, eliminating mask, pose, and warping modules at inference. At its core, a scene-task perceiver built on a Multimodal Large Language Model jointly encodes the source video, target garment, and task instruction into compact, task-aware latent tokens, implicitly capturing what to transfer and where and how to transfer it. A lightweight semantic bridge then aligns these tokens with the conditioning space of a diffusion-based video generator, enabling coherent garment transfer. To robustly couple the heterogeneous components, we devise a three-stage progressive training strategy comprising semantic alignment, joint task adaptation, and flexible-resolution refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UniVVT achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, validating implicit semantic guidance as a simple and effective alternative to fragile geometric preprocessing for end-to-end virtual try-on.

</details>


### [49] [GST-Bench: Can VLMs Develop Global Spatial Awareness from Video?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05747)
*Qifeng Zhang,Kaixiang Huang,Heng Dong,Huang Fang,Junting Chen,Junjie Zhu,Yonghang Chen,Zhiyu Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GST-Bench，一个用于视频理解中全局空间智能的VQA基准测试，包含6,790分钟合成视频生成的人工验证问题，要求模型从输入视频中未见过的新视角进行空间推理，并将自我中心观察映射到全局俯视图。对22个SOTA视觉语言模型的评估显示最强模型仅得42.68分，远低于人类79.08分，揭示模型在长时程全局空间一致性方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能基准仅关注从单个或少量视角的局部空间感知，忽视了在连续、长时程视觉流上的全局空间意识。具身智能体需要具备全局空间智能，因此需要一个能评估模型从长时程视频中构建全局空间表征能力的基准。

Method: 构建GST-Bench基准：从6,790分钟合成生成的视频中提取人工验证的问题，要求模型(1)从输入视频中未出现的新视角进行空间推理，(2)将自我中心观察映射到全局俯视图。同时构建GST-Bench-Local用于探测模型失败原因，以及GST-Train数据集作为全局空间推理的训练资源。

Result: 评估22个SOTA视觉语言模型后发现：最强零样本模型仅得分42.68，远低于人类79.08分。通过GST-Bench-Local进一步分析发现，模型在相同任务设定下局部空间理解能力强，但无法将长时程观测整合为全局一致性的场景表征。

Conclusion: 模型与人类在全局空间智能上存在显著差距，其核心瓶颈不在于局部空间理解能力，而在于长时程全局空间信息整合能力。GST-Train作为补充资源，旨在促进未来全局空间推理研究。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is fundamental to embodied agents, yet existing benchmarks focus on local spatial perception from single or few viewpoints, overlooking global spatial awareness over continuous, long-horizon visual streams. To address this limitation, we introduce the Global-Spatial-Temporal Benchmark (GST-Bench), a VQA benchmark for global spatial intelligence in video understanding, comprising human-verified questions derived from 6,790 minutes of synthetically generated video. It requires models to perform accurate spatial inference from novel viewpoints unseen in the input video and to map egocentric observations onto global top-down images. A comprehensive evaluation of 22 state-of-the-art VLMs exposes a striking gap between models and humans: the strongest zero-shot model attains only 42.68, far below the human score of 79.08. To probe the cause of this gap, we construct GST-Bench-Local and find that models, despite strong local spatial understanding under the same task formulation, still fail to consolidate long-horizon observations into a globally consistent scene representation. We further provide GST-Train, a dataset for global spatial reasoning, as a complementary resource to facilitate future research on this challenge.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Relevance: Bayesian Evidence Acquisition for Agentic Whole-Slide Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05757)
*Bryan Wong,Xun Xu,Huazhu Fu,Nancy F. Chen,Mun Yong Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEACON是一个即插即用的无训练智能体框架，将全切片图像（WSI）推理重构为贝叶斯证据获取问题，通过最大化期望信息增益（EIG）来顺序获取补丁以降低诊断不确定性，在五个WSI-VQA基准上取得最强整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练智能体框架基于语义相关性进行迭代补丁检索，但在计算病理学中，竞争性诊断往往具有相似且重叠的形态学模式，导致语义相关但诊断上无区分度的补丁被过度检索，产生冗余观察且无法解决诊断不确定性。

Method: BEACON将WSI推理建模为贝叶斯证据获取问题：维护对竞争诊断假设的概率信念，通过最大化期望信息增益（EIG）顺序获取补丁；配备证据控制器决定是直接回答、获取额外证据还是进行更高分辨率检查。整个框架完全由现成的基础模型构建，无需任何训练或微调。

Result: 在五个WSI-VQA基准上的零样本实验中，BEACON在无训练智能体框架中取得最强整体性能，同时显著提升了证据获取效率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯证据获取被确立为不确定性感知的智能体WSI推理的原则性范式，为病理诊断推理提供了更高效且更可靠的证据收集策略。

Abstract: Whole-slide image (WSI) reasoning requires an agent to sequentially acquire visual evidence before answering a diagnostic question. Existing training-free agentic frameworks formulate this process as iterative patch retrieval based on semantic relevance to the question. However, semantic relevance does not necessarily imply diagnostic informativeness in computational pathology, where competing diagnoses often exhibit similar and overlapping morphological patterns, making many patches semantically relevant yet diagnostically non-discriminative. Consequently, relevance-based retrieval may acquire redundant observations and leave diagnostic uncertainty unresolved. We propose BEACON, a plug-and-play agentic framework that reformulates WSI reasoning as a Bayesian evidence acquisition problem. BEACON maintains a probabilistic belief over competing diagnostic hypotheses and sequentially acquires patches by maximizing expected information gain (EIG) to reduce diagnostic uncertainty. An evidence controller then determines whether to answer, acquire additional evidence, or perform higher-resolution inspection. Built entirely from off-the-shelf foundation models, BEACON requires no additional training or fine-tuning. Extensive zero-shot experiments across five WSI-VQA benchmarks demonstrate that BEACON achieves the strongest overall performance among training-free agentic frameworks while substantially improving evidence acquisition efficiency, establishing Bayesian evidence acquisition as a principled paradigm for uncertainty-aware agentic WSI reasoning. The code is available at https://github.com/bryanwong17/BEACON

</details>


### [51] [Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05769)
*Zihao He,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FoRM（Relation Manifolds上的Flow-Map蒸馏），将基于关系的知识蒸馏重新表述为关系流形上的连续流映射问题，通过学习流映射算子Fθ(z,t,s)预测任意目标时间的关联状态，并结合安全半群一致性约束和端点锚定损失，在五个图像复原任务上取得优于SOTA蒸馏基线的效果，同时将训练方差降低约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法通常将教师与学生网络之间的中间特征或关系矩阵作为静态目标进行对齐，忽略了知识迁移过程的动态结构，无法充分利用轨迹级别的监督信息。

Method: 1) 将关系知识迁移建模为关系流形上的连续流映射问题；2) 学习流映射算子Fθ(z,t,s)，直接从当前时刻t的状态预测任意目标时刻s的关系状态，提供更丰富的轨迹级监督；3) 引入安全半群一致性约束，利用ground-truth桥接状态强制组合一致性，消除幻影状态误差累积；4) 引入端点锚定损失防止算子偏离教师目标。

Result: 在超分辨率、去雨、去噪、去模糊和低光增强五个图像复原任务上，跨多种骨干架构，均取得了优于SOTA蒸馏基线的性能提升，且与朴素流匹配蒸馏相比训练方差降低约50%。

Conclusion: FoRM通过将关系知识蒸馏提升为动态流映射学习问题，实现了更稳定、更高质量的图像复原蒸馏，验证了轨迹级监督与几何一致性约束的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation for image restoration typically aligns intermediate features or relation matrices between teacher and student networks as static targets, ignoring the dynamic structure of the knowledge transfer process. In this paper, we propose Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds (FoRM), which reformulates relation-based knowledge transfer as a continuous flow mapping problem on the relation manifold. Rather than regressing a constant velocity field between student and teacher relation states, FoRM learns a flow map operator $\mathcal{F}_θ(\mathbf{z}, t, s)$ that directly predicts the relation state at any target time $s$ given the current state at time $t$, enabling richer trajectory-level supervision. To ensure global self-consistency of the learned flow map, we introduce a safe semigroup consistency constraint that enforces compositional agreement using ground-truth bridge states, eliminating phantom-state error accumulation. An endpoint anchoring loss further prevents the operator from drifting away from the teacher target. Extensive experiments on five image restoration tasks, including super-resolution, deraining, denoising, deblurring, and low-light enhancement, demonstrate consistent gains over state-of-the-art distillation baselines across multiple backbone architectures, reducing training variance by approximately 50\% compared to naive flow matching distillation while achieving superior restoration quality.

</details>


### [52] [HyTBE: Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert Model for Cross-Domain Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05771)
*Aohua Li,Jin Kuang,Yubing Lu,Pingping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyTBE通过双曲几何建模目标-背景关系并利用专家适配器增强红外小目标检测的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测在域一致评估下已取得显著进展，但泛化到未见红外域时性能明显下降。现有方法依赖于有限源域中学习的目标-背景关系模式，当遇到训练中未出现的关系模式时判别能力减弱。作者将此问题形式化为"目标-背景关系偏移"（target-background relation shift）。

Method: 提出HyTBE模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) 目标-背景关系干预（Target-Background Relation Intervention），通过选择性扰动目标或背景来扩展训练中可观测的关系模式；(2) 双曲关系建模（Hyperbolic Relation Modeling），将多尺度视觉特征映射到Poincaré球中，根据特征token到目标和背景锚点的相对距离刻画目标-背景关系；(3) 双曲引导的MoE适配器（Hyperbolic-guided MoE Adapter），利用双曲关系表示校准多尺度视觉特征，并聚合针对不同关系模式的专家特征修正。

Result: 在NUAA-SIRST、NUDT-SIRST和IRSTD-1K上的留一域（leave-one-domain-out）实验中，HyTBE相比竞争基线取得了更强的跨域泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式关系线索扩展源域关系模式并自适应调整视觉表示，HyTBE有效解决了红外小目标检测中的目标-背景关系偏移问题，提升了跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) has achieved substantial progress under domain-consistent evaluation, yet detector performance often degrades markedly when generalizing to unseen infrared domains. Existing methods primarily improve detection by enhancing target responses and suppressing background interference. However, when trained on only a limited set of source domains, their learned decision rules are inevitably established from a restricted range of source-domain target-background relation patterns. We formulate this cross-domain failure as target-background relation shift: unseen domains may exhibit relation patterns that are not observed during training, thereby weakening the discriminative capability learned from the source domains. To address this problem, we propose HyTBE, a Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert model that expands source-domain relation patterns and adaptively adjusts visual representations using explicit relation cues. The Target-Background Relation Intervention selectively perturbs either targets or backgrounds, broadening the observable relation patterns during training while maintaining valid supervision. Subsequently, the Hyperbolic Relation Modeling maps multi-scale visual cues into a Poincaré ball and characterizes the target-background relation of each feature token according to its relative distances to the target and background anchors. The Hyperbolic-guided MoE Adapter further uses these hyperbolic relation representations to calibrate multi-scale visual features and aggregate expert-specific feature corrections for different relation patterns. Leave-one-domain-out experiments on NUAA-SIRST, NUDT-SIRST, and IRSTD-1K demonstrate that HyTBE achieves stronger cross-domain generalization than competitive baselines.

</details>


### [53] [SR-JEPA: Learning Predictive Latent State in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05774)
*Zihan Zhou,Qifu Wen,Xi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SR-JEPA，一种基于点的场景级点云JEPA架构，其冻结的预测通路可在指定位置查询。该模型在物体完全缺失的情况下，能从场景上下文推断出被移除物体的语义身份，在ARKitScenes上达到43.13%语义身份宏观准确率，并揭示了可查询、可组合的3D预测状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）通过预测缺失观测的潜在表征来学习，但现有遮蔽式JEPA的评估通常只关注其生成的编码器，而忽略了预测通路本身学到了什么。作者想探究：当场景中整个实体缺失时，训练好的预测通路能推断出什么信息？

Method: 引入SR-JEPA，一种点原生的场景级点云JEPA。预测通路以冻结形式在指定位置查询：评估时将某物体所有点移除，然后在质心处放置一个与形状无关的32点查询。训练仅使用自包含的3D EMA目标，不需要重建、语义标签、语言或2D特征。

Result: 在5,953个ARKitScenes物体上，补全潜表征达到43.13%语义身份宏观准确率，比最强基线高出22.18个百分点；随机化预测路径降低9.78个百分点，替换匹配的供体上下文降低21.98个百分点。在8,570个Sr3D支撑对上，完整潜表征达41.15 AP；从缺失物体潜表征解码的身份结合锚点身份与几何可达39.37 AP，剩余1.78个百分点的未解残差。

Conclusion: 该模型展现出可查询、可组合的3D预测状态：预测通路能够完成依赖上下文的实体内容，下游计算可将此与度量几何组合使用，验证了JEPA预测通路本身蕴含丰富的场景理解能力。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn by predicting latent representations of missing observations, yet many masked JEPAs are evaluated primarily through the encoders they produce. We ask what a trained predictive pathway itself infers when an entire entity is absent from a native 3D scene. We introduce SR-JEPA, a point-native JEPA for scene-scale point clouds whose original frozen predictive pathway can be queried at a supplied location. At evaluation, every point of one object is removed before encoding and replaced by the same shape-free 32-point query at its centroid. Training uses only self-contained 3D EMA targets: no reconstruction, semantic labels, language, or lifted 2D features. On 5,953 held-out ARKitScenes objects, the imputed latent reaches 43.13% semantic-identity macro accuracy, 22.18 points above the strongest floor. Randomizing the prediction path removes 9.78 points, while substituting matched donor context removes 21.98 points. On 8,570 Sr3D support pairs, the full latent reaches 41.15 AP; identity decoded from the missing-object latent, combined with anchor identity and geometry, reaches 39.37 AP, leaving an unresolved 1.78-point residual. These results reveal a queryable, compositional 3D predictive state: the model completes context-dependent entity content, which downstream computation combines with metric geometry.

</details>


### [54] [Vorch-Director: Interactive World Story Model via Noise-Aware Error Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05776)
*Lisai Zhang,Yidi Wu,Qi Liu,Xin Ma,Yang Ding,Gang Yue,Siqian Yang,Jingyuan Chen,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vorch-Director，一种噪声级别感知的残差校正策略，用于分钟级音视频自回归生成。通过将残差与来源噪声级别关联并在匹配噪声区间注入训练，缓解训练/推理分布不匹配，支持多镜头、多主体、参考引导的长视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归扩展是分钟级音视频生成的自然路径，但模型在干净的真实历史数据上训练，推理时使用自生成历史，导致累积误差引发身份漂移、过平滑和音视频不同步。现有方法通过复用预测残差作为合成噪声来缓解此问题，但残差校正的效果关键取决于产生残差时的流匹配（flow-matching）噪声级别，这一点未被充分认识。

Method: 1) 提出噪声级别感知的残差校正策略（Vorch-Director）：将每个残差关联其产生时的噪声级别，并在训练时注入来自匹配噪声区间的残差。2) 在音视频LTX-2扩散Transformer基础上引入任务嵌入（task embeddings），区分历史视频、参考图像和目标视频，实现统一的长时程生成条件化。3) 结合干净的条件汇（conditioning sink）和混合任务训练，支持多镜头、多主体、参考引导的长视频生成。

Result: 在ST-Bench上评估Vorch-Director，并引入一个新的长时程音视频基准（包含质量漂移和长程一致性指标）。大量实验表明，相比强基线方法，Vorch-Director在稳定性、音视频保真度上均有提升。

Conclusion: Vorch-Director通过将对齐注入误差与去噪过程相结合，能够在保持高效教师强制训练的同时产生更真实的自回归历史，为分钟级音视频生成提供了一种有效的稳定性解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive continuation provides a natural path toward minute-scale audio-visual generation by repeatedly extending a short-window generator conditioned on previously generated video and audio. However, models are trained on clean ground-truth histories, while inference relies on their own generated histories, where accumulated errors cause identity drift, over-smoothing, and audio-visual desynchronization. Recent methods reduce this mismatch by reusing prediction residuals as synthetic corruption, but we observe that the effectiveness of residual correction critically depends on the flow-matching noise level at which residuals are produced. We propose Vorch-Director, a noise-level-aware residual correction strategy that associates each residual with its originating noise level and injects residuals from matched noise regimes during training. By aligning injected errors with the denoising process, Vorch-Director produces more realistic autoregressive histories while retaining efficient teacher-forcing training. Built on the audio-visual LTX-2 diffusion transformer, Vorch-Director further introduces task embeddings to distinguish historical video, reference images, and target video, enabling unified conditioning for long-horizon generation. Together with a clean conditioning sink and mixed-task training, Vorch-Director supports multi-shot, multi-subject, reference-guided audio-visual long-video generation. We evaluate Vorch-Director on ST-Bench and introduce a new long-horizon audio-visual benchmark with metrics for quality drift and long-range consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved stability and audio-visual fidelity over strong baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Evidence-Driven Dynamic Visual Selector for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05780)
*Bo Zhang,Wenxin Wang,Feng Chen,Zhihao Zhang,Zixuan Wang,Changsheng Li,Yinjie Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EviSelect框架，利用目标MLLM内部注意力证据进行细粒度动态视觉选择，通过稀疏预填充高效探测视觉证据，指导分布感知的动态采样，在长视频理解中实现更优性能同时减少约50%视觉token并取得3.9倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM长视频理解方法依赖外部代理评分器和刚性启发式规则进行帧选择，与目标MLLM的内在证据不对齐，且无法适应视频中非均匀的时空信息密度分布。

Method: 1) 通过高度压缩的视觉输入和稀疏注意力近似目标MLLM的注意力图作为结构化先验；2) 基于三个互补的注意力组件设计轻量级选择器，同时定位查询相关时间戳并自适应调整局部采样率和空间分辨率；3) 将选择器建模为随机策略，通过GRPO在联合准确率-效率奖励下优化，鼓励策略根据视频信息密度动态分配计算。

Result: 在三个长视频理解基准上取得优于现有方法的性能，同时减少约50%的选定视觉token，实现3.9倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 基于目标MLLM内在注意力证据的动态视觉选择方法，能够有效地将计算资源分配到信息密度高的区域，在保持甚至提升性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，为长视频理解提供了更高效且更对齐的视觉选择范式。

Abstract: Recent advancements in MLLM-based long-form video understanding have mitigated inference-time computational cost and limited context lengths by selecting query-relevant frames. However, existing approaches predominantly rely on external proxy scorers and rigid heuristic rules, inevitably suffering from misalignment with the target MLLM's intrinsic evidence and failing to accommodate the non-uniform spatiotemporal information density. In this paper, we propose a fine-grained dynamic visual selection framework named EviSelect, grounded in the target MLLM internal attention evidence. Our method efficiently probes visual evidence via sparse prefilling as a structured prior to guide distribution-aware dynamic sampling. Specifically, we efficiently approximate attention maps of the target MLLM using highly compressed visual inputs and sparse attention, well-aligned to the full counterpart. Conditioned on three complementary attention components derived from this prior, we design a lightweight selector that not only precisely locates query-relevant timestamps but also adaptively adjusts the local sampling rate and spatial resolution. To enable evidence-conditioned spatiotemporal sampling, we formulate the selector as a stochastic policy and optimize it via GRPO under a joint accuracy--efficiency reward. By rewarding correct predictions under lower visual cost through group-relative comparisons, our method encourages the policy to allocate computation dynamically according to the information density of each video. Across three long video understanding benchmarks, EviSelect achieves superior performance compared to existing methods while reducing selected visual tokens by about 50\% and achieving a 3.9x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [56] [VSMP-IMU: Video-Grounded Semantic Motion Programs for Sensor-Aware Synthetic IMU Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05782)
*Lala Shakti Swarup Ray,Vitor Fortes Rey,Mengxi Liu,Paul Lukowicz,Bo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VSMP-IMU是一个基于视频的结构化语义运动程序（SMP）框架，用于可控合成IMU数据生成，解决可穿戴人体活动识别中标记数据稀缺的问题。它将活动定义语义与标签保持变异分离，从视频中提取并增强SMP，合成运动并转换为虚拟IMU信号，在五个公开数据集上取得平均Macro-F1 78.33%，超越真实训练9.77%和最强先前合成基线4.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴HAR受限于标记传感器数据稀缺，尤其是在低资源、类别不平衡和跨受试者泛化场景中。现有合成IMU方法存在权衡：视频驱动方法视觉基础好但易受姿态估计错误影响，文本驱动方法可控但缺乏对实际活动执行方式的语义基础。

Method: VSMP-IMU框架包括四个阶段：1) 从输入视频中提取结构化语义运动程序（SMP），将活动定义语义与标签保持变异分离；2) 对SMP进行增强以生成多样化的运动合成输入；3) 将合成运动转换为虚拟IMU信号；4) 将生成的信号对齐到目标可穿戴设备领域。

Result: 在五个公开IMU-HAR数据集上采用留一受试者评估，VSMP-IMU平均Macro-F1达78.33%，较真实训练提升9.77%，较最强合成基线提升4.04%。低资源场景下较真实训练提升18.54%，较最强基线提升超6%；长尾不平衡评估中，尾部类别Macro-F1较真实训练提升19.86%，较SOTA提升4.76%。

Conclusion: 结构化视频基础语义为可控、可穿戴相关的合成传感器数据生成提供了实用基础，验证了将活动语义与变化分离的SMP框架在增强HAR模型性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Wearable human activity recognition (HAR) is often limited by the scarcity of labeled sensor data, especially in low-resource, class-imbalanced, and subject-generalization settings. Synthetic IMU generation can reduce this dependency and enhance HAR machine learning model's performance, but existing approaches face a trade-off without addressing all factors: video-driven methods are visually grounded but sensitive to pose-estimation errors, while text-driven methods are controllable but often weakly grounded in how activities are actually performed. We present VSMP-IMU, a video-grounded framework for controllable synthetic IMU generation based on a structured Semantic Motion Program (SMP), which separates activity-defining semantics from label-preserving variation. Given an input video, VSMP-IMU extracts and augments an SMP, uses it to synthesize motion, converts the motion into virtual IMU signals, and grounds the resulting signals to the target wearable domain. We evaluate VSMP-IMU against state-of-the-art synthetic data generation methods on five public IMU-HAR datasets under leave-one-person-out evaluation. VSMP-IMU achieves an average Macro-F1 of 78.33%, improving over real-only training by 9.77% and over the strongest prior synthetic baseline by 4.04%. In low-resource settings with reduced training data-samples, it improves over real-only training by 18.54% and over the strongest prior synthetic baselines by more than 6% on average. Under long-tail evaluation in imbalanced datasets, it improves tail-class Macro-F1 by 19.86% over Real-only training and by 4.76% over SOTA. These results show that structured video-grounded semantics provide a practical foundation for controllable, wearable-relevant synthetic sensor data generation.

</details>


### [57] [KVAE: Family of Tokenizers for Multimodal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05798)
*Andrey Shutkin,Denis Parkhomenko,Ivan Kirillov,Kirill Chernyshev,Kirill Malakhov,Ilia Vasiliev,Ilia Trushkin,Valeriya Kobenko,David Chikovani,Alexander Ivanov,Azat Saginbaev,Egor Silvestrov,Ivan Mikheev,Konstantin Zakharov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一系列KVAE tokenizer（音频、图像、视频），用于后续文本条件下的生成任务，在重构和生成指标上均达到或超越现有前沿开源tokenizer，并公开了训练细节和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDM范式依赖tokenizer将输入信号映射为压缩表征，tokenizer直接决定生成质量、学习速度和下游应用基础，但目前缺乏统一的、面向多模态且性能卓越的开源tokenizer方案。

Method: 设计了三个系列的KVAE tokenizer：KVAE-Audio（48kHz全带宽连续音频tokenizer，50Hz潜在率、64通道）；KVAE-3D（两个因果视频tokenizer，支持4x16x16和4x8x8压缩）；KVAE-2D（图像模型，8倍压缩、32通道）。共享了训练细节、模型选择方法和设计消融。

Result: 在重构指标（PSNR、LPIPS、PESQ等）和生成指标（Frechet Distance、CLIP score、CLAP score等）以及主观侧对侧评估上，KVAE系列匹配或超越了Wan-2.2、HunyuanVideo-1.5、FLUX.2、MovieGen、StableAudio和MMAudio等前沿开源tokenizer。

Conclusion: KVAE系列为音频、图像和视频提供了一个统一的、高性能的tokenizer方案，可作为开源社区生成式模型训练的可靠基础设施。

Abstract: Latent diffusion modeling (LDM), a prominent paradigm, utilizes tokenizers to map input signal to compressed representation. This dependency positions tokenizer as an integral part of generation process itself, since it affects learning speed, quality of synthesized samples and lay foundation for later applications. This report presents series of KVAE tokenizers for audio, image and video, all designed for subsequent text-conditioned generation: KVAE-Audio, a continuous full-band 48 kHz tokenizer with a 50 Hz latent of 64 channels; KVAE-3D -- two causal video tokenizers for 4x16x16 and 4x8x8 compression; KVAE-2D, an image model, compressing input by factor of 8 with 32 channels. We demonstrate that reconstruction (PSNR, LPIPS, PESQ, etc.) and generation results on objective (Frechet Distance, CLIP score, CLAP score, etc.) and subjective (side-by-side evaluation) metrics matches or surpasses frontier opensource tokenizers, such as VAEs from Wan-2.2, HunyuanVideo-1.5, FLUX.2, MovieGen, StableAudio and MMAudio. Considering difficulty of development, we share with community training details, model selection method and ablation on design choices. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/kandinskylab/kvae and https://github.com/kandinskylab/kvae-audio.

</details>


### [58] [Vorch-Omni: Multi-Task Orchestration of Sight and Sound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05803)
*Vorch Team,Xiaoyu Chen,Yang Ding,Cong Han,Menglin Han,Yuxin Hong,Jiebo Hou,Zequn Jie,Xiang Li,Jing Liu,Qi Liu,Yulei Lu,Siyuan Luo,Lin Ma,Xin Ma,Yinlong Qian,Peng Shi,Fang Wan,Siqi Wang,Yaohui Wang,Yaole Wang,Yidi Wu,Siqian Yang,Mingyu Yin,Haoran Yu,Gang Yue,Lisai Zhang,Yuting Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vorch-Omni，一个基于"任意条件到任意输出"范式的统一多任务音频-视觉合成框架，通过单一流匹配扩散Transformer支持10余种任务（如文生视频、文生音视频、图像/参考条件生成、时序扩展、音频驱动生成、视频变换和音视频编辑等），无需任务特定的架构改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频建模方法通常依赖碎片化的任务特定模型，缺乏能够统一处理多种条件（目标、源、参考信号）和跨模态（音频+视觉）配置的通用模型。异构的目标、源和参考信号需要被区分以确定生成、保留或引导的内容，同时任务间干扰需要被减轻，而联合音视频生成进一步增加了跨模态条件和输出配置的复杂性。

Method: Vorch-Omni采用任意条件到任意输出的统一公式。具体包括：(1) 标记级条件掩码和任务标识符区分目标、源内容和参考；(2) 位置类型区分时序上下文与独立条件；(3) 互补的视觉条件通路——视觉语言模型解释采样帧与文本指令，视频VAE编码条件为潜在标记提供直接引导；(4) 分布式数据管线用于策展时序对齐的音视频片段、生成结构化标题和元数据、平衡异构任务分布。整个框架建立在单一流匹配扩散Transformer之上。

Result: 该框架无需任务特定的架构改动，统一支持超过10种任务，涵盖文生视频、文生音视频、图像和参考条件生成、时序扩展、音频驱动生成、视频变换及音视频编辑，展示了作为通用音频-视觉生成与操作可扩展基础的能力。

Conclusion: Vorch-Omni通过统一的公式和架构为通用音频-视觉生成提供了可扩展基础，有效解决了多任务、多模态条件下的任务干扰问题，为未来的通用生成模型研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video modeling have enabled diverse generation, reference-based synthesis, extension, and editing, but existing approaches often rely on fragmented task-specific models. A general model must distinguish heterogeneous target, source, and reference signals to determine what to generate, preserve, or use as guidance, while reducing interference among tasks. Joint audio-visual generation further increases this challenge by introducing diverse conditioning and output configurations across modalities. We present Vorch-Omni, a unified multi-task framework for audio-visual synthesis based on an arbitrary-condition-to-arbitrary-output formulation. It flexibly treats video and audio signals as either conditioning inputs or generation targets. Token-level conditioning masks and task identifiers distinguish targets, source content, and references, while position types separate temporal context from independent conditions. To capture semantic and structural information, Vorch-Omni employs complementary visual conditioning pathways: a vision-language model interprets sampled frames with text instructions, and a video VAE encodes conditions into latent tokens for direct guidance. We further build a distributed data pipeline to curate diverse temporally aligned audio-visual clips, generate structured captions and metadata, and balance heterogeneous task distributions. Built on a single flow-matching diffusion transformer without task-specific architectural changes, Vorch-Omni supports over 10 tasks, including text-to-video, text-to-audio-video, image- and reference-conditioned generation, temporal extension, audio-driven generation, video transformation, and audio-visual editing. This unified framework provides a scalable foundation for general-purpose audio-visual generation and manipulation.

</details>


### [59] [Ordered Diffusion for 3D Human Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05804)
*Mattia Masiero,Ilya A. Petrov,Daniel Cremers,Gerard Pons-Moll,Riccardo Marin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ODin是一种基于3D扩散过程的生成式人体配准方法，将配准建模为对齐分布而非回归任务，在保持模板语义的同时生成与目标几何对齐的点云，不仅超越回归基线，还以三分之二的推理时间节省成为新的最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维人体配准传统上被视为回归任务，假设输入点云与模板之间存在唯一的地面真值对齐。然而，采集噪声、遮挡和未知软组织动态导致人体扫描存在固有歧义，回归方法收敛到平均预测，往往无法代表合理的几何形状。

Method: ODin将配准建模为3D扩散过程，通过生成一个与目标几何对齐且保持模板语义（一致点序）的点云来解决歧义。方法依赖全局、局部和位置三类条件引导每个点到达正确位置。

Result: 实验表明，这种生成式方法不仅优于回归基线，还超越了精心设计的传统方法，并实现了新的最先进性能，同时将配准时间减少三分之二。

Conclusion: 通过拥抱配准中的不确定性并将其建模为分布，生成式扩散框架能产生更合理的对齐结果，且效率更高，展示了生成式配准范式的优越性。

Abstract: 3D human registration has historically been treated as a regression task, assuming a unique ground-truth alignment exists between the template and an input point cloud. In reality, acquisition noise, occlusions, and unknown soft tissue dynamics introduce inherent ambiguity into human scans. Regression-based methods consequently converge to an average prediction, often failing to represent a plausible geometry. In our work, we embrace such uncertainty by modeling the registration as a distribution of alignments. We propose ODin, which formulates registration as a 3D diffusion process that generates a point cloud aligned with the target geometry while preserving template semantics through consistent point ordering. To achieve this, ODin relies on global, local, and positional conditioning, guiding each point to its correct location. Our experiments demonstrate that such a generative formulation not only outperforms its regression-based baseline, but also establishes a new state of the art, surpassing highly engineered methods while reducing the registration time by two-thirds. Pre-trained models and code are available at https://riccardomarin.github.io/odin/.

</details>


### [60] [STAIL: Semantic Text-Anchored Incremental Learning for Medical Imaging via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05808)
*Songpan Gao,Yajie Zhang,Guanxing Chen,Jiayu Qian,Zhenzhen Liu,Shijun Li,Xiaowei Zhu,Yao Hu,Kay Chen Tan,Yu-An Huang,Shiqi Wang,Zhi-An Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAIL框架，借鉴人类认知机制，利用语义文本锚定（文本描述+冻结大语言模型）替代传统的像素级图像回放，以极低存储代价实现医学图像增量学习中旧任务的密集语义重建，并有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分析中的深度学习模型在动态环境中持续适应新临床任务时存在严重的灾难性遗忘问题。主流增量学习方法通过回放原始历史图像来缓解，但这种像素级回放存在三大缺陷：存储开销大、引发隐私问题、稀疏样本难以捕捉真实数据分布。

Method: STAIL包含两个核心组件：1) 非对称语义整合缓冲区（SCB）——仅保存少量图像锚点和大量文本描述，以最小存储代价实现旧任务的高密度语义重建；2) 基于大语言模型的语义锚定机制（LSAM）——利用冻结LLM的稳定语义空间作为发展先验，在宏观和微观层面将演化的视觉特征显式锚定到文本表示，同时约束模型的可塑性和稳定性。

Result: 在眼底、超声和X射线三类异构医学数据集上的大量实验表明，STAIL作为即插即用模块全面提升了多种现有基线的性能，实现了AAA-AUC平均提升2.24%（持续性）和BWT-AUC平均提升3.55%（遗忘减少）。

Conclusion: STAIL通过语义文本锚定替代传统像素级回放，在极小存储成本下有效缓解医学图像增量学习中的灾难性遗忘，兼具可扩展性和即插即用性，代码已公开。

Abstract: Deep learning models applied to medical image analysis suffer from severe catastrophic forgetting when continually adapting to new clinical tasks in dynamic environments. Mainstream incremental learning methods typically mitigate this by rehearsing raw historical images. However, this pixel-level rehearsal incurs significant storage overhead, raises privacy concerns, and fails to adequately capture the true data distribution with sparse exemplars. Inspired by human cognitive mechanisms, we propose a novel framework termed Semantic Text-Anchored Incremental Learning (STAIL) for sequential clinical tasks. To overcome the rehearsal bottleneck, STAIL introduces an asymmetric semantic consolidation buffer (SCB). By incorporating a minimal set of image anchors and extensive textual descriptions, the SCB enables dense semantic reconstruction of old tasks at a minimal storage cost. Furthermore, we design an LLM-derived Semantic Anchoring Mechanism (LSAM) that leverages the stable semantic space of frozen large language models as developmental priors. This mechanism explicitly anchors evolving visual features to textual representations, guiding and constraining plasticity and stability at both macroscopic and microscopic levels. Extensive experiments across three heterogeneous medical datasets, covering fundus, ultrasound, and X-ray imaging, demonstrate that STAIL acts as a highly effective plug-and-play module. It comprehensively enhances the performance of various existing baselines, achieving average gains of 2.24\% in AAA-AUC for sustained performance and 3.55\% in BWT-AUC for reduced forgetting. Code is available.

</details>


### [61] [Energy-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05811)
*Haoyang Tong,Yu He,Fang Li,Lichen Ma,Jingling Fu,Dong Chen,Zhen Chen,Junshi Huang,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出能量引导流匹配（EG-FM），通过将固定的干净图像端点替换为基于热核滤波的移动端点，显式建模从低频到高频的粗到细生成轨迹，实现无需修改骨干网络和训练数据的零成本改进，在ImageNet 256×256类条件生成上达到FID 1.45，512×512下FID 1.58，并在文本到图像生成上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间生成模型需要在高维空间中联合学习全局结构与细粒度细节，标准流匹配将噪声插值到固定干净图像端点，导致频谱演化需要隐式学习，缺乏对生成轨迹中低频到高频结构的显式约束。

Method: EG-FM将流匹配的固定端点替换为热核滤波后的移动端点，该端点从低频图像平滑演化到干净图像；通过图像特定的能量引导调度释放移动端点中的高频信号比例，从而重新定向流匹配的速度场。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256类条件生成任务上，200个epoch达到FID 1.55，600个epoch达到FID 1.45；在512×512分辨率下仅40个高分辨率适应epoch即取得FID 1.58；在文本到图像生成任务上GenEval得分0.85、DPG-Bench得分83.9。

Conclusion: EG-FM通过显式建模粗到细的频谱演化轨迹，以可忽略的训练和推理开销显著提升流匹配生成质量，且无需修改骨干网络或训练数据，具有很好的通用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Pixel-space generative models bypass lossy latent compression, yet necessitate joint learning of global structure and fine-grained details in a high-dimensional space. Standard flow matching interpolates noise toward a fixed clean-image endpoint, leaving the spectral evolution to be learned implicitly. In this paper, we introduce Energy-Guided Flow Matching(EG-FM) that explicitly models a coarse-to-fine generative trajectory by moving endpoint. Specifically, EG-FM replaces the fixed endpoint with a heat-kernel-filtered endpoint that evolves smoothly from low-frequency image to clean image.The fraction of high-frequency signal in moving endpoint is released by an image-specific energy-guided scheduling, leading to the re-targeting of velocity in flow matching.Our framework requires no adaptation of the backbone and training data, bringing negligible cost on the training and inference stages. In our experiment, EG-FM consistently achieves lower FID on the ImageNet class-conditional image generation task at $256 \times 256$ with fewer epochs, reaching an FID of 1.55 at 200 epochs and 1.45 at 600 epochs. We continue training the generation task on the setting of $512 \times 512$ resolution, yielding a FID of 1.58 after only 40 high-resolution adaptation epochs.Furthermore, we transfer EG-FM on text-to-image generation and achieve 0.85 on GenEval score and 83.9 on DPG-Bench. Code is available at https://github.com/ysng123/EG-FM.

</details>


### [62] [Bayesian adaptively-weighted ensembles for few-shot abdominal segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05815)
*Abbas Al-Sabbagh,Shalom F. Mushtaq,Tomás M. da Silva,Kushagra Soni,Binawei Gbamila,Sri Atluri,Qianye Yang,Yipeng Hu,Claire C. Villette,Shaheer U. Saeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯自适应加权集成框架，用于标签稀缺和域偏移场景下的解剖结构分割。通过贝叶斯优化自动确定多个少样本分割算法的集成权重，在跨机构男性盆腔数据集上验证了显著优于个体学习器、固定权重集成等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本学习算法在解剖分割中各有优缺点，性能因解剖目标和机构而异。现有的少样本分割集成方法采用固定权重方案，无法根据目标域动态调整模型贡献，因此需要一个能够自适应调节权重以适应目标域的方法。

Method: 1) 先使用小型标注支持集适配多个少样本分割算法；2) 利用贝叶斯优化在目标域验证集上自动搜索最优集成权重，最大化分割性能；3) 固定学习到的权重，应用于未见过的目标域查询图像进行预测合并。

Result: 在跨机构男性盆腔数据集上，使用留出的解剖结构和机构模拟同时的标签稀缺和机构域偏移场景。实验表明该方法相较于个体少样本学习器、固定权重集成、从头训练基线和近期SOTA集成方法均有统计学显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将模型贡献自适应于目标解剖结构和机构域，该框架为在严重标注约束下将分割系统部署到新临床站点提供了实用机制。

Abstract: Few-shot learning has emerged as a promising approach for anatomical segmentation when labelled data are scarce. However, different few-shot learning algorithms exhibit complementary strengths and weaknesses, with performance varying across anatomical targets and institutions. Existing few-shot segmentation ensembles, that combine predictions from multiple algorithms, typically employ fixed weighting schemes and therefore cannot adjust model contributions according to the target domain. In this work, we propose a Bayesian adaptively-weighted ensemble framework for segmentation under label scarcity and domain shift. Multiple few-shot segmentation algorithms are first adapted using a small labelled support set. Bayesian optimisation is then used to automatically identify ensemble weights that maximise segmentation performance on a target-domain validation set. The learned weights are subsequently fixed and applied to combine predictions on previously unseen query images from the target domain. The proposed framework is evaluated on the Cross-institution Male Pelvic Structures dataset using held-out anatomical structures and institutions to simulate simultaneous label scarcity and institutional domain shift. Results demonstrate statistically significant improvements over individual few-shot learners, fixed-weight ensembles, training-from-scratch baselines and recent state-of-the-art ensembling approaches. By adapting model contributions to the target anatomy and institutional domain, the proposed framework provides a practical mechanism for deploying segmentation systems to new clinical sites under severe annotation constraints.

</details>


### [63] [Controllable Clothing: Precise Labels and Generation for Virtual Try-On with Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05834)
*Max Rehman Linder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于虚拟试穿（VITON）图像生成的新方法，利用开源AI模型为服装图像标注长度和风格等标签，通过训练适配器实现用户可控的多样化图像生成，确保最终生成的试穿图像尽可能真实反映服装的实际版型，避免误导消费者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在虚拟试穿场景中，生成的图像往往与真实服装的版型、长度和风格存在偏差，可能误导消费者。现有方法缺乏对生成结果的有效控制，需要一种能够精确还原服装真实属性的图像生成方法。

Method: 该方法利用新的开源AI模型对图像数据进行标签增强（如长度、风格等），并训练适配器（adapters）将标签与服装图像配对，从而实现对生成图像的多样化控制和精确引导。

Result: 通过标签与服装图像的配对训练，模型能够生成更多样化且用户可控的图像，使零售端用户可以确保生成图像与真实服装版型高度一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够提升虚拟试穿图像的真实性和可控性，有助于零售商向消费者呈现更准确的服装展示效果，降低误导消费者的风险。

Abstract: In this technical report, I present a new method for guiding image generation in the context of Virtual- Try-On (VITON). The proposed method leverages new open source Ai models to augment the image data with labels, such as lengths and styles. By training adapters with these labels paired with images of the garments, the model can produce a more diverse set of images that the user can control. For the end user, such as a retailer, this means that they can assure that the produced image is as true to the true fit as possible, not misleading consumers

</details>


### [64] [Accurate Localization of Road Traffic Objects on the Road Plane Using Surveillance Camera Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05840)
*Jan Gawroński,Witold Czajewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段几何感知定位管道，用于单目路侧监控相机下的车辆本地化。首先使用YOLO26检测车辆，然后通过ResNet34回归网络预测车辆在地面上的投影四角点，最终以四边形几何中心作为车辆位置。在合成数据(CARLA)上训练并在真实数据(DAIR-V2X)上微调，相比朴素边界框中心定位方法，图像空间定位误差从31.77px降至15.30px（提升51.8%），中等距离车辆地面误差从5.52m降至0.90m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准方法通常使用检测器边界框中心来估计车辆位置，但透视畸变和视差会导致较大误差，尤其在相机架设较高且车辆体积较大的场景下。需要一种几何感知的方法来克服这一局限性。

Method: 两阶段管道：(1) 使用YOLO26检测器检测车辆；(2) 使用ResNet34回归网络预测车辆底座投影的四个角点，最终位置由预测四边形的几何中心计算得出。在CARLA合成数据上训练，并在DAIR-V2X真实路侧数据上微调。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X上，图像空间平均定位误差从31.77px降至15.30px（51.8%提升），中位误差降至4.29px。中等距离车辆地面中位误差从5.52m降至0.90m，远距离车辆从8.67m降至1.84m。远距离和几何困难场景（强透视畸变和视差）下改善最大。

Conclusion: 检测器边界框周围的上下文信息对几何定位至关重要；预测车辆底座投影四边形的方法能显著优于边界框中心法，尤其适用于路侧监控场景下受透视畸变和视差影响大的车辆。

Abstract: Accurate vehicle localization from monocular roadside surveillance cameras is important for intelligent transportation systems, traffic monitoring, and traffic conflict analysis. Standard approaches often estimate vehicle position from the center of the detector bounding box, which can produce large errors due to perspective distortion and parallax, especially for elevated cameras and large vehicles.
  This paper proposes a two-stage geometry-aware localization pipeline that estimates the projection of the vehicle footprint onto the road plane. First, vehicles are detected using a YOLO26-based detector. Second, a dedicated ResNet34 regression network predicts four corner points corresponding to the projected vehicle base. The final position is computed as the geometric center of the predicted quadrilateral.
  The method was trained on synthetic data generated in CARLA and fine-tuned on real-world roadside imagery from DAIR-V2X. Experiments on synthetic and real data showed clear improvements over naive bounding-box-center localization. On DAIR-V2X, the mean image-space localization error decreased from 31.77 px to 15.30 px, a 51.8% improvement, while the median error decreased to 4.29 px. Median ground-plane error for medium-range vehicles decreased from 5.52 m to 0.90 m, and for far-range vehicles from 8.67 m to 1.84 m.
  The results also show that contextual information surrounding the detector bounding box is important for geometric localization. The largest gains were observed for distant vehicles and geometrically challenging cases affected by strong perspective distortion and parallax.

</details>


### [65] [Overcoming Attention Drift: Homogeneity-Heterogeneity Guided Feature Aggregation for Low-Light Remote Sensing Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05843)
*Yaozi Zhong,Xingxing Yang,Shaohui Mei,Mingyang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HALO，一个基于双先验驱动的遥感图像极低光增强框架，利用光照不变语义先验和伪3D拓扑先验分别提供区域同质性正偏置和边界异质性负惩罚，并通过H2CAM模块协同融合这两个先验，在8个合成与真实遥感基准上达到SOTA性能，显著改善物理边界清晰度和色彩保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端低光条件下遥感图像受严重噪声与光照退化影响，现有方法在特征聚合时存在注意力漂移问题，错误地在不同物理边界之间聚合特征，导致结构模糊和色彩失真，影响下游地球观测任务。

Method: 提出HALO双先验驱动增强框架：(1) 光照不变语义先验提供区域同质性作为正偏置，促进内容一致的特征聚合；(2) 伪3D拓扑先验提供边界异质性作为负惩罚，防止跨边界混淆；(3) 提出同质性-异质性协同注意力模块(H2CAM)解决跨模态先验融合时的特征冲突。

Result: 在8个具有挑战性的合成和真实遥感基准上达到SOTA性能，显著提升物理边界清晰度和色彩保真度，同时最大化保留下游地球观测任务的判别性特征。

Conclusion: HALO通过双先验（同质性正偏置+异质性负惩罚）的协同引导，有效解决了低光遥感增强中的注意力漂移问题，实现了更优的边界清晰度、色彩保真度和特征保留，为极端低光条件下的遥感图像增强提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Restoring high-fidelity remote sensing imagery from extreme low-light degradation is indispensable for reliable Earth observation and downstream machine vision. However, under severe noise and illumination corruption, existing methods suffer from attention drift, erroneously aggregating features across distinct physical boundaries and causing severe structural blurring and color distortion. To address this, we propose HALO, a dual-prior-driven enhancement framework that formulates enhancement as a guided feature aggregation problem driven by foundation model priors. Specifically, an illumination-invariant semantic prior provides regional homogeneity as a positive bias for content-consistent aggregation, while a pseudo-3D topological prior provides boundary heterogeneity as a negative penalty to strictly prevent cross-boundary confusion. To cooperatively incorporate these two priors, we propose a Homogeneity-Heterogeneity Cooperative Attention Module (H2CAM) to resolve feature conflicts during cross-modal prior fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HALO achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 challenging synthetic and real-world remote sensing benchmarks, significantly improving physical boundary sharpness and color fidelity while maximizing the preservation of discriminative features for downstream Earth observation tasks.

</details>


### [66] [Curia-MAE: Multi-Modal Multi-Anatomy MAE Pre-Training for 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05844)
*Théo Danielou,Antoine Saporta,Léo Alberge,Corentin Dancette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Curia-MAE，一种在30万CT和MRI图像上预训练的多模态、多解剖部位掩码自编码器（MAE）医学影像基础模型。通过扩展卷积MAE预训练（加入鲁棒重建目标、特征正则化和局部-全局相似性目标），在8个解剖和病灶分割基准上，冻结编码器性能超越强MAE基线，与全微调相比保持竞争力，并在病灶任务（标注数据稀缺）上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学基础模型虽然能通过冻结编码器加小层适配新任务，但稠密预测任务（如3D分割）在其评估中代表性不足，且冻结编码器时预训练模型仍不及从零训练的nnU-Net。作者旨在缩小这一差距，验证单一冻结编码器能否跨多种分割任务复用，降低临床工作流中适配和部署成本。

Method: 在卷积MAE预训练基础上扩展了三个组件：(1) 鲁棒重建目标；(2) 特征正则化器；(3) 局部-全局相似性目标。模型在300,000张CT和MRI图像上预训练，覆盖大量解剖部位，实现多模态、多解剖部位预训练。

Result: 在8个解剖和病灶分割基准上，Curia-MAE在冻结编码器设置下优于强MAE基线；在全微调下保持竞争力；在病灶分割任务（标注数据稀缺场景）上表现更优。

Conclusion: 单一冻结编码器可以跨多种分割任务复用，显著降低临床工作流中模型适配和部署成本。预训练模型权重将公开发布。

Abstract: Radiology foundation models learn transferable representations that can be adapted to new tasks by training only small layers on top of a frozen encoder. Dense prediction tasks such as 3D segmentation are, however, underrepresented in their evaluation, and, with the encoder kept frozen, pre-trained models still fall short of nnU-Net, the state-of-the-art reference trained from scratch. To close this gap we extend convolutional MAE pre-training with a robust reconstruction objective, a feature regularizer, and a local-global similarity objective. Using this method, we propose Curia-MAE, a multi-modal, multi-anatomy MAE model pre-trained on 300,000 CT and MRI images covering a large number of anatomical sites. On eight anatomy- and lesion-focused segmentation benchmarks, Curia-MAE improves frozen-encoder performance over a strong MAE baseline, while remaining competitive under full finetuning and superior on lesion tasks, where labeled data is scarce. These results indicate that a single frozen encoder can be reused across diverse segmentation tasks, reducing the cost of adapting and deploying such models in clinical workflows. We will make our pre-trained model weights publicly available.

</details>


### [67] [DTRNet: Dual Text-Radical Decoding for Handwritten Chinese Text Recognition with Faked Character Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05848)
*Runrui Li,Lin Zhu,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DTRNet，一种双文本-部首解码框架，用于K-12教育场景中的行级伪造汉字检测。该框架将上下文感知的文本识别与逐字结构验证解耦，通过文本分支进行行级转录、部首分支预测合法的表意文字描述序列（IDS）来实现基于词典的伪造字符判断，并引入IDS引导的置信度调整（IGCA）在推理阶段用结构证据优化文本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12教育场景中，手写汉字识别不仅要转录学生书写，还要检测伪造字符。现有识别模型受限于预定义正常字符集，无法显式识别伪造字符；现有检测方法存在互补性局限：字符级方法提供可解释的结构证据但效率低，行级方法效率高但依赖置信度分数，容易漏检且缺乏显式结构证据。关键挑战在于在保持行级效率的同时，保留独立于上下文推理的字符结构证据。

Method: DTRNet采用双分支解耦架构：文本分支负责行级转录（上下文感知），部首分支预测合法的IDS序列用于基于词典的伪造字符判定。此外引入IDS引导置信度调整（IGCA）机制，在推理阶段利用结构证据细化文本预测结果。

Result: 实验结果表明DTRNet在有效检测伪造字符的同时保持了较强的识别性能，并提供可解释的部首级证据。

Conclusion: DTRNet成功实现了行级效率与字符级结构证据的结合，为K-12手写汉字伪造检测提供了同时具备高效性和可解释性的解决方案，且代码、检查点和处理后的数据集均已公开。

Abstract: In K-12 educational scenarios, handwritten Chinese text recognition should not only transcribe student writing, but also detect faked characters. However, existing recognition models are usually confined to a predefined set of normal characters and therefore cannot explicitly identify faked characters. Existing detection methods exhibit complementary limitations: character-level methods provide interpretable structural evidence but suffer from low efficiency, whereas line-level methods are efficient but rely heavily on confidence scores, making them prone to missed detections and lacking explicit structural evidence. Thus, the key challenge is to preserve character-structural evidence independent of contextual inference while maintaining line-level efficiency. To this end, we propose DTRNet, a dual Text-Radical decoding framework for line-level faked character detection. DTRNet decouples context-aware text recognition from character-wise structural verification, where the text branch performs line-level transcription and the radical branch predicts legal Ideographic Description Sequences (IDS) for lexicon-based faked character judgment. We further introduce IDS-Guided Confidence Adjustment (IGCA) to refine text predictions using structural evidence during inference. Experimental results demonstrate that DTRNet effectively detects faked characters while maintaining strong recognition performance and providing interpretable radical-level evidence. Code, checkpoints, and the processed dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/BNU-ERC-ITEA/DTRNet.

</details>


### [68] [Shape-Aware Oriented Bounding Box (OBB) to Horizontal Bounding Box (HBB) Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05858)
*Badha Rathna Sabhapathy,Gotam Dahiya,Vishesh Vatsal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向遥感图像中船舶检测的形状感知OBB转HBB方法，利用船体形状、饱满度和边界框方向，生成更紧凑的轴对齐水平边界框，减少空隙和数据丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像中物体（如船舶、飞机）具有空间朝向，OBB比HBB更贴合且NMS更稳健，但现有OBB转HBB的后处理方法要么引入大量空白背景，要么丢失检测数据，缺乏一种既能保持紧凑性又不损失信息的转换方案。

Method: 提出一种形状感知的OBB-to-HBB转换方法，核心是利用船体形状（hull shape）、船体饱满度（hull fullness）以及边界框朝向信息，生成更紧致的轴对齐HBB表示。

Result: 所提方法在OBB转HBB转换中与三种基线方法（Outer HBB最小最大法、Area Equivalent HBB面积等价法、GBB Marginalized HBB）进行基准对比，生成更紧致的HBB。

Conclusion: 形状感知的OBB转HBB方法能有效减少转换过程中的空白背景引入，生成更紧凑的HBB表示，适用于遥感船舶检测。

Abstract: Accurate object detection in aerial and satellite imagery is dependent upon the bounding box representation. This is especially true for spatially oriented objects such as ships or aircrafts.
